  "implementation_pseudocode": "LAYOUT (workspace root = cwd): src/labeller/{fol.py,repair_census.py,labeller.py}, src/screen.py, src/disguise.py, src/rewrites.py, src/judges.py, src/roundtrip.py, src/pilot_metrics.py, src/analysis.py, src/budget.py, prereg.json, data/screen_items.json, data/invariance_items.json, data/disguise_map.json, results/{scores_*.jsonl, costs.jsonl, method_out.json, tables/*.csv}, logs/. Follow aii-python (uv, loguru, pathlib), aii-parallel-computing (ProcessPool for z3 with per-call timeouts; asyncio+aiohttp with a Semaphore(16) for OpenRouter), aii-long-running-tasks (mini → 20 → 100 → full). Every OpenRouter call goes through budget.py.\n\nSTEP 0: BUDGET GUARD (src/budget.py)\n  class Budget: cap_total=9.5, per_component caps {judge_cheap_primary:1.2, judge_cheap_secondary:0.6, roundtrip:1.0, gold_recall_probe:0.3, prompt_dev:0.3, strong:5.0}\n  call(model, messages, **kw): before the call, estimate cost = (len(prompt_tokens)*price_in + max_tokens*price_out); refuse if spent+est > cap. After the call, read usage (prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, and usage.cost if OpenRouter returns it, requesting usage accounting with extra_body={'usage':{'include':True}}). Append a line to results/costs.jsonl {ts, component, model, item_id, in_tok, out_tok, cost}. Update spent and persist it to results/budget_state.json after every call.\n  Retries: 3 with exponential backoff on 429/5xx; JSON-parse failure → one retry with 'Return ONLY the JSON object'; then mark the call as a metric failure.\n  Prices come from the OpenRouter /api/v1/models listing via aii-openrouter-llms, fetched at start and saved to results/prices.json.\n\nSTEP 1: LABELLER (src/labeller/)\n  copy fol.py and repair_census.py. In fol.py set PREC = {'iff':1,'xor':3,'imp':2,'or':3,'and':4}, so ⊕ binds like ∨, tighter than →.\n  Unit tests: parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp',('xor',A,B),C); parse('A → B → C') is right-assoc; parse('∀x (P(x)) ∧ ∀y (Q(y))') gives two quantified conjuncts.\n  labeller.py:\n    def equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand_str, ref_str, ms=5000) -> dict:\n      try parse both, else return {cls:'UNPARSEABLE'}\n      r = equivalent(c, ref); if r is True: return {cls:'EQUIV'}; if r is None: remember timeout\n      ao, pmap, cmap = align(c, ref); if equivalent(ao, ref) is True: return {cls:'VOCAB', pmap, cmap, borderline: any(token_jaccard(p,q) < 0.5 for renames)}\n      gb = gran_bridge(ao, ref); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True: return {cls:'GRAN', borderline: True}\n      ops, dt = search(gb[0] if gb else ao, gb[1] if gb else ref, budget_s=25)\n      if ops: return {cls:'ERROR', ops, depth:len(ops)}\n      return {cls:'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'} if every equivalence call timed out else {cls:'COMPOUND'}\n  Regression check: rerun on the 99 track-H non-equivalent pairs and diff against iter-3 repair_census.rows.json. Log every changed row, expecting the ⊕/→ bracketing COMPOUNDs to change. Save results/labeller_regression.json.\n  Run all labelling in a ProcessPoolExecutor (spawn, 6 workers, per-item wall timeout of 60 s → TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN).\n\nSTEP 2: SHARED SCREEN (src/screen.py), exactly as the strategy defines it\n  norm(s) = lowercase, unify quotes/dashes, strip all punctuation, collapse whitespace.\n  download the 3 Logic-LM files from raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{sys}.json (fallback: git clone --depth 1, or the GitHub contents API). Save them under data/logiclm/.\n  parse_program(raw_logic_programs[0]): split on the 'Predicates:', 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:' headers. Each line splits at ' ::: ' into (fol, nl). Keep the predicate glosses in the record, NOT shown to judges. A malformed program → every sentence of that story gets candidate_fol=None → UNPARSEABLE.\n  curated conclusions: rows whose id starts with 'concl_' → {nl, ref=FOL_sentence, orig=FOL_sentence_old, amb=ambiguity}.\n  TRACK L conclusions: for each (sys, story, conclusion line) whose norm(nl) equals the norm of a curated conclusion → reference = corrected FOL_sentence.\n  TRACK L premises: for each Logic-LM premise, find a curated story premise with an equal norm(nl). Split the curated story NL paragraph into sentences with a regex splitter; align sentence i to FOL_sentence_old line i ONLY when the counts match (log the stories where they do not). Also look up the folio-refined 'nl premises'/'fol premises' line with the same norm(nl). Accept it as a trusted reference only if equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig_line, refined_line) ∈ {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}; reference = the original line.\n  MATCH-RATE rule: report n_distinct_conclusions_matched, n_premises_matched and the rates. Include premise items only as the strategy specifies: ALWAYS include matched agreed premises as track-L items with ref_type='agreed_premise' (the strategy's track-L definition includes them). If fewer than 150 distinct conclusions matched, ALSO add the matched stories' agreed premises (the same set, so the rule is automatically satisfied), and log the case. Use no other source.\n  label track L: eq = equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand, ref) → label = CORRECT if eq.cls in {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}; ERROR if eq.cls=='ERROR'; UNCERTAIN if eq.cls in {COMPOUND, TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN}; UNPARSEABLE if eq.cls=='UNPARSEABLE'. Store auto_class, repair_ops and vocab_borderline.\n  TRACK H: the 202 curated conclusions + 100 MALLS-test rows; candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected, system='human_original', and the sub-source kept.\n    If equivalent(orig, ref) → CORRECT. Otherwise run eq as above. If amb is True and non-equivalent modulo vocab → READING_CHOICE. Otherwise map as in track L (VOCAB/GRAN → CORRECT_VOCAB, a sub-flag of CORRECT that is also reported separately). Orig unparseable → UNPARSEABLE.\n    The census class for per-type sensitivity is eq.ops (single-op/two-op) or COMPOUND.\n  item_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol_raw.strip())[:16]\n  strata, computed from the TEXT and the REFERENCE formula (candidate-independent): words = len(text.split()) → bin '<12' | '12-19' | '>=20'; n_quant = count of ∀/∃ in the reference; depth = AST depth of the reference; n_cond = number of conjuncts in the antecedents of → and in the restrictors of ∀ in the reference; exception = regex r'\\b(unless|except|excluding|other than|apart from|but not|without)\\b' on the text, storing the marker.\n  story context (for the pilot metrics): the same system's premises for the story (track L); the curated FOL_sentence_old lines for the story (FOLIO track H); none for MALLS.\n  write data/screen_items.json, sorted by item_id: {item_id, track, system, story_id, role, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, auto_class, repair_ops, vocab_borderline, ambiguity, census_class, strata, story_premises_fol}.\n  Also write data/label_vector.sha1 = sha1 of the sorted 'item_id:label' lines, so iteration 2 can check byte-identity with experiments A/B/C.\n\nSTEP 3: INVARIANCE SET (src/rewrites.py)\n  base = the first 150 track-L CORRECT items by item_id (if fewer than 150 exist, use all and top up with track-H CORRECT items by item_id, logged).\n  families, seed 0:\n    RENAME: each predicate and constant → a WordNet synonym of its head word (else the name + 'Thing'), same arity. Verify with equivalence under the inverse map.\n    REORDER: shuffle the conjuncts/disjuncts of every ∧/∨ node.\n    DEMORGAN: rewrite one ¬(A∧B) ↔ ¬A∨¬B, or introduce ¬¬ on an ∧ node.\n    PRENEX: pull quantifiers out where the variables do not clash.\n    CONTRAPOSITIVE: A→B becomes ¬B→¬A.\n  Keep a rewrite only if it is z3-equivalent to its base (RENAME: modulo the known map) and its string differs. Record per-family applicability counts. Rewrites are scored by every baseline, including the disguised judge; they need no labels.\n  write data/invariance_items.json {rw_id, base_item_id, family, text, candidate_fol}.\n\nSTEP 4: DISGUISE (src/disguise.py)\n  PROTECTED = logic, quantifier and function words: all, every, each, some, any, no, none, not, never, if, then, only, unless, except, either, or, and, nor, both, neither, is, are, be, a, an, the, who, which, that, there, exists, also, may, can, must, does, do, of, in, on, at, to, for, with, by, from, as, than, more, most, least, many, few, one, two, three, their, they, it, its, he, she, his, her, someone, something, everyone, everything, people, person, thing (the last four are kept: sortal nouns).\n  content lemmas: spaCy en_core_web_sm POS in {NOUN, PROPN, VERB, ADJ, ADV} and not in PROTECTED, plus the camelCase/underscore pieces of every predicate and constant name in the candidate (lowercase, lemmatised with spaCy's lemmatizer).\n  nonce(lemma, seed_text) = a pronounceable CV(C)CV(C)CV string from a fixed syllable inventory (consonants 'bdfgklmnprstvz', vowels 'aeiou'), indexed by int(sha1(norm(seed_text)+'|'+lemma),16). Reject and re-hash if the string is an English word (nltk words corpus) or collides with another lemma in the item.\n    seed_text = norm(text), so the same sentence gets the same mapping in all 3 Logic-LM systems' items and in its rewrites.\n  text: replace each content token by its nonce, carrying the inflection over by simple suffix rules (-s, -es, -ed, -ing, -er, -est, -ly). Capitalise it if the token was capitalised (proper nouns).\n  formula: predicate name → CamelCase concatenation of the mapped pieces, with protected pieces kept as-is (e.g. PerformOftenIn → BlikorTadumIn). Constant → lowercase concatenation. Arity, argument order, variables and connectives are untouched. Operate on the AST, re-serialise with a printer that round-trips through fol.parse.\n  ASSERTIONS, run on ALL items; the 30 sha1-first items are also printed to results/disguise_audit.txt:\n    (i) the disguised formula parses, and applying the inverse name map gives an AST identical to the original's;\n    (ii) the multiset of arities is unchanged;\n    (iii) same lemma → same nonce in text and formula; distinct lemmas → distinct nonces;\n    (iv) residual leak rate = the fraction of original content lemmas (length ≥4) still present in the disguised text or formula, which must be 0 for the formula; the text rate is reported;\n    (v) every PROTECTED word count in the text is unchanged.\n  A failing item keeps judge_*_disg = metric failure (flagged) and is counted.\n  write data/disguise_map.json.\n\nSTEP 5: PRE-REGISTRATION (prereg.json, written and sha1-hashed BEFORE any score is joined to a track-L label)\n  contents: model ids and prices; prompt texts and their sha1; temperature 0; max_tokens; the strong-judge subsample item_ids; the score orientation of every metric (higher = more likely unfaithful); the operating thresholds; the baseline combination feature sets; the bootstrap settings (2,000 resamples, cluster = norm(text), seed 0); the tie-margin and stratum-testability rule (≥50 per class, otherwise 'descriptive only'); the vocab_borderline sensitivity analysis.\n  Operating thresholds, fixed on TRACK H CORRECT items only (never track L):\n    judges: flag if P(faithful) < 0.5;\n    continuous non-LLM or round-trip metrics: flag if the score is worse than the 10th percentile of their values on track-H CORRECT items (a 10% FA target);\n    binary metrics: their natural value.\n\nSTEP 6: JUDGES (src/judges.py)\n  PRIMARY CHEAP JUDGE (the pre-registered bar 'judge_cheap'): google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (fallbacks in order: google/gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001, qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct-2507, verified with aii-openrouter-llms). A non-OpenAI family avoids self-preference on GPT-generated track-L candidates.\n  SECONDARY CHEAP JUDGE: openai/gpt-4.1-nano with logprobs=True, top_logprobs=5, max_tokens=1. It uses a minimal variant of the same rubric ending 'Answer with one word: YES if faithful, NO otherwise.' P(faithful) = exp(lp_YES)/(exp(lp_YES)+exp(lp_NO)), summed over the case/space variants of YES/NO. If logprobs are absent, fall back to the verdict (0/1) and log it.\n  RUBRIC (system prompt, fixed): 'You check whether a first-order logic formula faithfully expresses the meaning of an English sentence. Predicate and constant names may differ from the words of the sentence; logically equivalent rewritings are acceptable; judge MEANING only: quantifiers, which things are conditions vs asserted properties, negation, argument order, and whether any content is missing or added.' Variant B appends the census definitions: NEG negation wrong/missing; REV implication direction reversed; QUANT wrong quantifier; RESTR restrictor misplaced (e.g. ∀x(A∧B) where ∀x(A→B) is meant, or ∃x(A→B)); CONN wrong connective (∧/∨/⊕/→/↔); MOVE content attached to the wrong part; DROP a condition or content missing; ADD content not in the sentence; SWAP arguments in the wrong order; BIND variable wrongly bound or free; SCOPE quantifier scope wrong; UNGLUE independent claims wrongly merged or split; COMPOUND several of these; NONE.\n  User message: 'Sentence: {text}\\nFormula: {fol}\\nReturn JSON: {\"faithful_prob\": <0-100>, \"verdict\": \"FAITHFUL\"|\"UNFAITHFUL\", \"error_type\": <one code from the list, NONE if faithful>, \"reason\": \"<=25 words\"}'. Primary judge: response_format json_object where supported, temperature 0, max_tokens 150.\n  PROMPT DEV (the fair-tuning step): run variants A and B of the primary judge on a 60-item dev slice = the 60 sha1-first MALLS track-H items (CORRECT + ERROR). Pick the variant with the higher AUROC (tie → B, because B yields type labels) and freeze it in prereg.json. The dev slice is excluded from all track-H headline numbers and reported separately. Track L is never used. Cost ≈ $0.02.\n  SWEEPS, async with Semaphore(16), staged mini(20) → 100 → full:\n    primary judge: every track-L item + every track-H item (non-UNPARSEABLE; unparseable candidates are still judged, because the judge sees raw text, and scored) × {orig, disg} + all rewrite items × {orig, disg}.\n    secondary judge: the same set.\n    Cost check after the mini stage: extrapolate to the full sweep and abort if the extrapolation exceeds the component cap. Expected ≈ (≈700 L + 302 H + ≈700 rewrites) × 2 ≈ 3.4k calls × ~$0.00015 ≈ $0.5 per cheap judge.\n  STRONG JUDGE: choose by a measured 10-call dry run among openai/gpt-5 (reasoning effort 'low') and google/gemini-2.5-pro or gemini-3-pro-preview (thinking budget ~1k). NOT Anthropic, xAI or GLM: those families are dataset artifact E's adjudication panel. Pick the lowest measured $/call that returns valid JSON on 10/10. Same rubric variant; the score is the JSON faithful_prob.\n    subsample (pre-registered): 200 track-L items from CORRECT/ERROR, allocated equally across label × length-bin cells (6 cells; take cell sizes min(avail, 34), redistributing the shortfall to the largest cells), chosen by item_id order within cells; plus 100 track-H items, 50 ERROR (1- or 2-op and COMPOUND proportional) + 50 CORRECT, excluding the dev slice. Each item is run orig AND disg (600 calls).\n    If the dry-run $/call × 600 > $5.0: first lower the reasoning effort ('minimal') or the thinking budget; then shrink to 150 L + 70 H. Always keep the orig/disg pairing.\n  GOLD-RECALL PROBE (contamination, direct): the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this sentence into first-order logic in FOLIO notation: {text}' for every track-H FOLIO conclusion and MALLS item (302 calls, ≈$0.03). Measure: the rate of z3-equivalence to the ORIGINAL public gold vs to the corrected gold, and the exact predicate-name-set Jaccard with the original gold vs with the Logic-LM gpt-4 output for the same sentence. Name overlap far above the Logic-LM baseline indicates memorised gold.\n  per-item fields: judge_cheap_orig, judge_cheap_disg (P(faithful) in [0,1]), judge_cheap_type, judge_cheap_type_disg, judge_cheap2_orig/disg, judge_strong_orig/disg, judge_*_fail flags, cost_usd, seconds.\n\nSTEP 7: ROUND-TRIP (src/roundtrip.py)\n  verbalise: the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this first-order logic formula into one plain English sentence. Say exactly what the formula says, including odd or unnatural structure; do not fix it. Read CamelCase predicate names as words. Formula: {fol}'. T=0, max_tokens 120. It runs on every track-L and track-H item and on the rewrites (rewrites are needed for the invariance FA).\n  NLI: MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on GPU, fp16, batch 32. p_fwd = P(entail | premise=text, hyp=verbalisation), p_bwd = P(entail | premise=verbalisation, hyp=text). rt_nli_min = min(p_fwd, p_bwd); also store rt_nli_fwd, rt_nli_bwd and rt_nli_contra = max contradiction. Robustness checkpoint: cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-large, reported as rt_nli_min_alt.\n  embedding: sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2 cosine(text, verbalisation) → rt_embed_cos.\n  re-formalise: the same cheap model gets 'Write a first-order logic formula (symbols ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕) for this sentence, using ONLY these predicates/constants: {signature of the candidate: names with arities}. Sentence: {verbalisation}'. Then rt_reformalise_eq = 1 if equivalent_modulo_vocab(reformalised, candidate).cls in {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}, 0 if not, and a failure if unparseable (flagged). This is the gold-free round-trip certification of 2604.25031, run as a baseline.\n  cost ≈ 2 × ~1.7k calls × $0.0001 ≈ $0.35.\n\nSTEP 8: PILOT STRUCTURAL METRICS (src/pilot_metrics.py)\n  unzip the user's pilot into pilot_ref/ with zipfile and read its metric1/2/3 code or docs. Write results/pilot_mapping.md stating each original definition and its FOL adaptation. If the zip lacks one of them, use the definitions below.\n  For item i with story context S (the same system's other formulas in the story; for track-H FOLIO, the original gold premise lines; MALLS items have no story and get only the candidate-internal parts):\n    pilot_joint_conflict = 1 if z3 finds S ∪ {cand} UNSAT (timeout 5 s → NA-flagged); also emit pilot_story_unsat = 1 if S alone is UNSAT.\n      CONFOUND: a FOLIO conclusion labelled False is legitimately inconsistent with its premises. Report this metric's AUROC overall AND within FOLIO answer-label strata, as a diagnostic only; the answer label is not used by the metric.\n    pilot_arity_incons = the number of predicate names in cand used with an arity different from their use in S, plus arity clashes within cand.\n    pilot_dangling = the fraction of cand's predicates and constants that appear nowhere else in S (for conclusions: 'used but never defined by the premises'). Also emit pilot_undeclared = cand predicates missing from the system's own 'Predicates:' declaration list (Logic-LM only; the closest analogue of the user's 'used but not defined').\n    pilot_rerun_jacc = the mean Jaccard of cand's predicate-name set (lowercased, camelCase-split tokens) with the other two Logic-LM systems' candidates for the same sentence. Track L only. A PROXY for rerun stability, logged as such.\n  PARSE RATE: parse_ok = fol.parse succeeds (and the z3 conversion succeeds). It is the only metric defined on UNPARSEABLE items; report the parse rate per system.\n\nSTEP 9: ANALYSIS (src/analysis.py), all per-item scores oriented so that higher = more likely unfaithful\n  metric list M = [parse_fail, pilot_joint_conflict, pilot_arity_incons, pilot_dangling, pilot_undeclared, 1-pilot_rerun_jacc, 1-rt_nli_min, 1-rt_nli_fwd, 1-rt_nli_bwd, 1-rt_embed_cos, 1-rt_reformalise_eq, 1-judge_cheap_orig, 1-judge_cheap_disg, 1-judge_cheap2_orig, 1-judge_cheap2_disg, 1-judge_strong_orig, 1-judge_strong_disg]. Metric failures are set to the max (flagged) and counted in coverage.\n  PRIMARY: track L, CORRECT(0) vs ERROR(1), UNCERTAIN excluded. For each m: AUROC (sklearn, ties 0.5), AUPRC with its prevalence baseline, tie rate among (error, correct) pairs, and at the prereg threshold precision, recall, FA and balanced accuracy, plus precision reweighted to 10% and 25% prevalence. CIs: cluster bootstrap over norm(text), 2,000 resamples, seed 0, percentile 95%.\n  SECONDARY: (a) track H (dev slice excluded), CORRECT vs ERROR with READING_CHOICE and UNCERTAIN excluded; (b) track L with vocab_borderline moved to UNCERTAIN; (c) track L ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN vs CORRECT (a pessimistic label view); (d) the strong-judge subset, where every metric is recomputed on those same items so the comparisons are paired.\n  PAIRED DELTAS: ΔAUROC for judge_cheap_disg vs every other metric, and judge_strong_orig vs judge_cheap_orig/disg on the subset (cluster bootstrap on the same resamples). Also DeLong as a check.\n  CONTAMINATION: for each judge and track, Δ_disg = AUROC_disg − AUROC_orig with a paired CI. DiD = Δ_disg(track H) − Δ_disg(track L) with a paired CI (bootstrap resampling both tracks independently). Also the change in the mean judge score on track-H ERROR items (public original gold), orig vs disg: memorised gold should push orig scores UP. Plus the gold-recall probe rates. Interpretation, pre-registered: contamination evidence = DiD CI < 0, or a recall-probe original-gold name-Jaccard significantly above the Logic-LM baseline.\n  BEST BASELINE COMBINATION: 5-fold GroupKFold (groups = norm(text)), L2 logistic regression (C=1.0, standardised features, missing → 0 plus a missing-indicator), cross-fitted on track L CORRECT/ERROR. Feature sets (prereg): S1 structural = parse/pilot*; S2 round-trip = rt_*; S3 judges = judge_cheap_disg + judge_cheap2_disg; S4 all cheap = S1∪S2∪S3∪judge_cheap_orig; S5 = S4 + judge_strong_* (subset only). Report each set's OOF AUROC with a CI. 'Best' = the max OOF AUROC among S1-S4, reported with a note that selecting among 4 sets is mildly optimistic. Emit per-item OOF predictions baseline_combo_S{k}_oof and best_baseline_oof, so iteration 2 can compute nested ΔAUROC for candidates A/B/C with the SAME folds (fold assignment saved as data/folds.json keyed by item_id). Repeat the fitting on track H (fold-split) as a secondary.\n  PER-TYPE: on track H ERROR items with census classes, the sensitivity of each metric at its threshold per census operator (items with multiple ops count toward each) and for COMPOUND. For the judges' type labels: accuracy = judge_cheap_type ∈ census ops (single-op items, and 2-op items), plus a confusion table. Same for track-L ERROR items with auto ops.\n  INVARIANCE: for each metric and rewrite family, FA = the fraction of rewrites flagged at the threshold, and flip rate = flagged(rewrite) ≠ flagged(base). Also the mean |score(rewrite) − score(base)|. The judges are scored orig and disg.\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA per words bin, n_quant (0-1/2/≥3), depth, n_cond (0/1-2/≥3), exception (y/n), on tracks L and H. Cells with <50 per class are marked 'descriptive'. A logistic slope test of judge correctness vs words: judge_cheap_disg correct at threshold ~ z(words) + label.\n  SYSTEM LEVEL: per Logic-LM system, the true error rate (labels) vs each metric's flagged rate and mean score. With 3 systems this is descriptive only; no τ test.\n  COVERAGE: per metric, the fraction of items scored without failure, by track and system; the UNPARSEABLE count per system; the label-class distribution per system (CORRECT/ERROR/UNCERTAIN/UNPARSEABLE) and the vocab_borderline count.\n  COST: per metric, the mean $ per item (from costs.jsonl) and seconds per item (wall time per item for LLM calls; the GPU NLI batch time amortised per item; z3 CPU seconds).\n\nSTEP 10: OUTPUTS\n  results/method_out.json: follow the aii-json exp_gen_sol_out schema (validate it; generate the mini/preview variants). Each example carries the per-item fields {item_id, track, system, label, auto_class, repair_ops, census_class, strata, parse_ok, pilot_joint_conflict, pilot_story_unsat, pilot_arity_incons, pilot_dangling, pilot_undeclared, pilot_rerun_jacc, rt_verbalisation, rt_nli_min, rt_nli_fwd, rt_nli_bwd, rt_nli_contra, rt_embed_cos, rt_reformalise_eq, judge_cheap_orig, judge_cheap_disg, judge_cheap_type, judge_cheap2_orig, judge_cheap2_disg, judge_strong_orig, judge_strong_disg, best_baseline_oof, fold, cost_usd, seconds, coverage_status}. A top-level 'analysis' block holds every table above, plus match rates, the labeller regression diff, disguise audit stats, the prereg sha1 and total spend.\n  Also results/rewrite_scores.jsonl, results/tables/*.csv, results/summary.md (a plain-language table of AUROC [CI] per baseline, the contamination verdict, the best-combo AUROC and the bar statement: 'candidates must beat judge_cheap_disg AUROC=X and best_baseline_oof AUROC=Y').\n  Write README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml (keep: results/, data/; delete (redownloadable): the HF model cache hf_cache/ with source 'huggingface-cli download MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2 cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-large'; .venv/ regenerable via 'uv sync'). Set HF_HOME=./hf_cache so the cache stays inside the workspace.",
  "fallback_plan": "F1, Logic-LM download fails: try git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM, then the GitHub contents API. If all fail, track L cannot be built. Then run everything on track H only, mark 'track L unavailable' at the top of summary.md, and still emit screen_items.json with track H, so the label vector stays joinable.\nF2, low match rate: if fewer than 60 track-L CORRECT+ERROR items remain after labelling (likely if Logic-LM's coarse predicates push many items into UNCERTAIN), do NOT add other sources (strategy rule). Instead (i) still score every UNCERTAIN item, so dataset E's adjudicated labels can re-admit them in iteration 2; (ii) report analysis (c) (ERROR∪UNCERTAIN vs CORRECT) prominently, labelled pessimistic; (iii) make track H the powered track for this artifact and say so.\nF3, OpenRouter model unavailable or price changed: use the pre-listed fallbacks (primary: gemini-2.5-flash-lite → gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001 → qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct; secondary: gpt-4.1-nano → gpt-5-nano with reasoning minimal → mistral-small with a score output; strong: gpt-5 low → gemini-2.5-pro → gemini-3-pro-preview). Record the swap in prereg.json BEFORE running.\nF4, budget pressure: the priority order when a cap binds is (1) primary cheap judge orig+disg on track L, (2) the same on track H, (3) the strong judge on the subset (shrink it before dropping it), (4) round-trip on track L, (5) the secondary cheap judge, (6) rewrites (score them with the primary judge only), (7) the gold-recall probe. Capitalise it if the token was capitalised (proper nouns).\\n  formula: predicate name → CamelCase concatenation of the mapped pieces, with protected pieces kept as-is (e.g. PerformOftenIn → BlikorTadumIn). Constant → lowercase concatenation. Arity, argument order, variables and connectives are untouched. Operate on the AST, re-serialise with a printer that round-trips through fol.parse.\\n  ASSERTIONS, run on ALL items; the 30 sha1-first items are also printed to results/disguise_audit.txt:\\n    (i) the disguised formula parses, and applying the inverse name map gives an AST identical to the original's;\\n    (ii) the multiset of arities is unchanged;\\n    (iii) same lemma → same nonce in text and formula; distinct lemmas → distinct nonces;\\n    (iv) residual leak rate = the fraction of original content lemmas (length ≥4) still present in the disguised text or formula, which must be 0 for the formula; the text rate is reported;\\n    (v) every PROTECTED word count in the text is unchanged.\\n  A failing item keeps judge_*_disg = metric failure (flagged) and is counted.\\n  write data/disguise_map.json.\\n\\nSTEP 5: PRE-REGISTRATION (prereg.json, written and sha1-hashed BEFORE any score is joined to a track-L label)\\n  contents: model ids and prices; prompt texts and their sha1; temperature 0; max_tokens; the strong-judge subsample item_ids; the score orientation of every metric (higher = more likely unfaithful); the operating thresholds; the baseline combination feature sets; the bootstrap settings (2,000 resamples, cluster = norm(text), seed 0); the tie-margin and stratum-testability rule (≥50 per class, otherwise 'descriptive only'); the vocab_borderline sensitivity analysis.\\n  Operating thresholds, fixed on TRACK H CORRECT items only (never track L):\\n    judges: flag if P(faithful) < 0.5;\\n    continuous non-LLM or round-trip metrics: flag if the score is worse than the 10th percentile of their values on track-H CORRECT items (a 10% FA target);\\n    binary metrics: their natural value.\\n\\nSTEP 6: JUDGES (src/judges.py)\\n  PRIMARY CHEAP JUDGE (the pre-registered bar 'judge_cheap'): google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (fallbacks in order: google/gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001, qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct-2507, verified with aii-openrouter-llms). A non-OpenAI family avoids self-preference on GPT-generated track-L candidates.\\n  SECONDARY CHEAP JUDGE: openai/gpt-4.1-nano with logprobs=True, top_logprobs=5, max_tokens=1. It uses a minimal variant of the same rubric ending 'Answer with one word: YES if faithful, NO otherwise.' P(faithful) = exp(lp_YES)/(exp(lp_YES)+exp(lp_NO)), summed over the case/space variants of YES/NO. If logprobs are absent, fall back to the verdict (0/1) and log it.\\n  RUBRIC (system prompt, fixed): 'You check whether a first-order logic formula faithfully expresses the meaning of an English sentence. Predicate and constant names may differ from the words of the sentence; logically equivalent rewritings are acceptable; judge MEANING only: quantifiers, which things are conditions vs asserted properties, negation, argument order, and whether any content is missing or added.' Variant B appends the census definitions: NEG negation wrong/missing; REV implication direction reversed; QUANT wrong quantifier; RESTR restrictor misplaced (e.g. ∀x(A∧B) where ∀x(A→B) is meant, or ∃x(A→B)); CONN wrong connective (∧/∨/⊕/→/↔); MOVE content attached to the wrong part; DROP a condition or content missing; ADD content not in the sentence; SWAP arguments in the wrong order; BIND variable wrongly bound or free; SCOPE quantifier scope wrong; UNGLUE independent claims wrongly merged or split; COMPOUND several of these; NONE.\\n  User message: 'Sentence: {text}\\\\nFormula: {fol}\\\\nReturn JSON: {\\\"faithful_prob\\\": <0-100>, \\\"verdict\\\": \\\"FAITHFUL\\\"|\\\"UNFAITHFUL\\\", \\\"error_type\\\": <one code from the list, NONE if faithful>, \\\"reason\\\": \\\"<=25 words\\\"}'. Primary judge: response_format json_object where supported, temperature 0, max_tokens 150.\\n  PROMPT DEV (the fair-tuning step): run variants A and B of the primary judge on a 60-item dev slice = the 60 sha1-first MALLS track-H items (CORRECT + ERROR). Pick the variant with the higher AUROC (tie → B, because B yields type labels) and freeze it in prereg.json. The dev slice is excluded from all track-H headline numbers and reported separately. Track L is never used. Cost ≈ $0.02.\\n  SWEEPS, async with Semaphore(16), staged mini(20) → 100 → full:\\n    primary judge: every track-L item + every track-H item (non-UNPARSEABLE; unparseable candidates are still judged, because the judge sees raw text, and scored) × {orig, disg} + all rewrite items × {orig, disg}.\\n    secondary judge: the same set.\\n    Cost check after the mini stage: extrapolate to the full sweep and abort if the extrapolation exceeds the component cap. Expected ≈ (≈700 L + 302 H + ≈700 rewrites) × 2 ≈ 3.4k calls × ~$0.00015 ≈ $0.5 per cheap judge.\\n  STRONG JUDGE: choose by a measured 10-call dry run among openai/gpt-5 (reasoning effort 'low') and google/gemini-2.5-pro or gemini-3-pro-preview (thinking budget ~1k). NOT Anthropic, xAI or GLM: those families are dataset artifact E's adjudication panel. Pick the lowest measured $/call that returns valid JSON on 10/10. Same rubric variant; the score is the JSON faithful_prob.\\n    subsample (pre-registered): 200 track-L items from CORRECT/ERROR, allocated equally across label × length-bin cells (6 cells; take cell sizes min(avail, 34), redistributing the shortfall to the largest cells), chosen by item_id order within cells; plus 100 track-H items, 50 ERROR (1- or 2-op and COMPOUND proportional) + 50 CORRECT, excluding the dev slice. Each item is run orig AND disg (600 calls).\\n    If the dry-run $/call × 600 > $5.0: first lower the reasoning effort ('minimal') or the thinking budget; then shrink to 150 L + 70 H. Always keep the orig/disg pairing.\\n  GOLD-RECALL PROBE (contamination, direct): the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this sentence into first-order logic in FOLIO notation: {text}' for every track-H FOLIO conclusion and MALLS item (302 calls, ≈$0.03). Measure: the rate of z3-equivalence to the ORIGINAL public gold vs to the corrected gold, and the exact predicate-name-set Jaccard with the original gold vs with the Logic-LM gpt-4 output for the same sentence. Name overlap far above the Logic-LM baseline indicates memorised gold.\\n  per-item fields: judge_cheap_orig, judge_cheap_disg (P(faithful) in [0,1]), judge_cheap_type, judge_cheap_type_disg, judge_cheap2_orig/disg, judge_strong_orig/disg, judge_*_fail flags, cost_usd, seconds.\\n\\nSTEP 7: ROUND-TRIP (src/roundtrip.py)\\n  verbalise: the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this first-order logic formula into one plain English sentence. Say exactly what the formula says, including odd or unnatural structure; do not fix it. Read CamelCase predicate names as words. Formula: {fol}'. T=0, max_tokens 120. It runs on every track-L and track-H item and on the rewrites (rewrites are needed for the invariance FA).\\n  NLI: MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli on GPU, fp16, batch 32. p_fwd = P(entail | premise=text, hyp=verbalisation), p_bwd = P(entail | premise=verbalisation, hyp=text). rt_nli_min = min(p_fwd, p_bwd); also store rt_nli_fwd, rt_nli_bwd and rt_nli_contra = max contradiction. Robustness checkpoint: cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-large, reported as rt_nli_min_alt.\\n  embedding: sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2 cosine(text, verbalisation) → rt_embed_cos.\\n  re-formalise: the same cheap model gets 'Write a first-order logic formula (symbols ∀ ∃ ¬ ∧ ∨ → ↔ ⊕) for this sentence, using ONLY these predicates/constants: {signature of the candidate: names with arities}. Sentence: {verbalisation}'. Then rt_reformalise_eq = 1 if equivalent_modulo_vocab(reformalised, candidate).cls in {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}, 0 if not, and a failure if unparseable (flagged). This is the gold-free round-trip certification of 2604.25031, run as a baseline.\\n  cost ≈ 2 × ~1.7k calls × $0.0001 ≈ $0.35.\\n\\nSTEP 8: PILOT STRUCTURAL METRICS (src/pilot_metrics.py)\\n  unzip the user's pilot into pilot_ref/ with zipfile and read its metric1/2/3 code or docs. Write results/pilot_mapping.md stating each original definition and its FOL adaptation. If the zip lacks one of them, use the definitions below.\\n  For item i with story context S (the same system's other formulas in the story; for track-H FOLIO, the original gold premise lines; MALLS items have no story and get only the candidate-internal parts):\\n    pilot_joint_conflict = 1 if z3 finds S ∪ {cand} UNSAT (timeout 5 s → NA-flagged); also emit pilot_story_unsat = 1 if S alone is UNSAT.\\n      CONFOUND: a FOLIO conclusion labelled False is legitimately inconsistent with its premises. Report this metric's AUROC overall AND within FOLIO answer-label strata, as a diagnostic only; the answer label is not used by the metric.\\n    pilot_arity_incons = the number of predicate names in cand used with an arity different from their use in S, plus arity clashes within cand.\\n    pilot_dangling = the fraction of cand's predicates and constants that appear nowhere else in S (for conclusions: 'used but never defined by the premises'). Also emit pilot_undeclared = cand predicates missing from the system's own 'Predicates:' declaration list (Logic-LM only; the closest analogue of the user's 'used but not defined').\\n    pilot_rerun_jacc = the mean Jaccard of cand's predicate-name set (lowercased, camelCase-split tokens) with the other two Logic-LM systems' candidates for the same sentence. Track L only. A PROXY for rerun stability, logged as such.\\n  PARSE RATE: parse_ok = fol.parse succeeds (and the z3 conversion succeeds). It is the only metric defined on UNPARSEABLE items; report the parse rate per system.\\n\\nSTEP 9: ANALYSIS (src/analysis.py), all per-item scores oriented so that higher = more likely unfaithful\\n  metric list M = [parse_fail, pilot_joint_conflict, pilot_arity_incons, pilot_dangling, pilot_undeclared, 1-pilot_rerun_jacc, 1-rt_nli_min, 1-rt_nli_fwd, 1-rt_nli_bwd, 1-rt_embed_cos, 1-rt_reformalise_eq, 1-judge_cheap_orig, 1-judge_cheap_disg, 1-judge_cheap2_orig, 1-judge_cheap2_disg, 1-judge_strong_orig, 1-judge_strong_disg]. Metric failures are set to the max (flagged) and counted in coverage.\\n  PRIMARY: track L, CORRECT(0) vs ERROR(1), UNCERTAIN excluded. For each m: AUROC (sklearn, ties 0.5), AUPRC with its prevalence baseline, tie rate among (error, correct) pairs, and at the prereg threshold precision, recall, FA and balanced accuracy, plus precision reweighted to 10% and 25% prevalence. CIs: cluster bootstrap over norm(text), 2,000 resamples, seed 0, percentile 95%.\\n  SECONDARY: (a) track H (dev slice excluded), CORRECT vs ERROR with READING_CHOICE and UNCERTAIN excluded; (b) track L with vocab_borderline moved to UNCERTAIN; (c) track L ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN vs CORRECT (a pessimistic label view); (d) the strong-judge subset, where every metric is recomputed on those same items so the comparisons are paired.\\n  PAIRED DELTAS: ΔAUROC for judge_cheap_disg vs every other metric, and judge_strong_orig vs judge_cheap_orig/disg on the subset (cluster bootstrap on the same resamples). Also DeLong as a check.\\n  CONTAMINATION: for each judge and track, Δ_disg = AUROC_disg − AUROC_orig with a paired CI. DiD = Δ_disg(track H) − Δ_disg(track L) with a paired CI (bootstrap resampling both tracks independently). Also the change in the mean judge score on track-H ERROR items (public original gold), orig vs disg: memorised gold should push orig scores UP. Plus the gold-recall probe rates. Interpretation, pre-registered: contamination evidence = DiD CI < 0, or a recall-probe original-gold name-Jaccard significantly above the Logic-LM baseline.\\n  BEST BASELINE COMBINATION: 5-fold GroupKFold (groups = norm(text)), L2 logistic regression (C=1.0, standardised features, missing → 0 plus a missing-indicator), cross-fitted on track L CORRECT/ERROR. Feature sets (prereg): S1 structural = parse/pilot*; S2 round-trip = rt_*; S3 judges = judge_cheap_disg + judge_cheap2_disg; S4 all cheap = S1∪S2∪S3∪judge_cheap_orig; S5 = S4 + judge_strong_* (subset only). Report each set's OOF AUROC with a CI. 'Best' = the max OOF AUROC among S1-S4, reported with a note that selecting among 4 sets is mildly optimistic. Emit per-item OOF predictions baseline_combo_S{k}_oof and best_baseline_oof, so iteration 2 can compute nested ΔAUROC for candidates A/B/C with the SAME folds (fold assignment saved as data/folds.json keyed by item_id). Repeat the fitting on track H (fold-split) as a secondary.\\n  PER-TYPE: on track H ERROR items with census classes, the sensitivity of each metric at its threshold per census operator (items with multiple ops count toward each) and for COMPOUND. For the judges' type labels: accuracy = judge_cheap_type ∈ census ops (single-op items, and 2-op items), plus a confusion table. Same for track-L ERROR items with auto ops.\\n  INVARIANCE: for each metric and rewrite family, FA = the fraction of rewrites flagged at the threshold, and flip rate = flagged(rewrite) ≠ flagged(base). Also the mean |score(rewrite) − score(base)|. The judges are scored orig and disg.\\n  COMPLEXITY: AUROC and FA per words bin, n_quant (0-1/2/≥3), depth, n_cond (0/1-2/≥3), exception (y/n), on tracks L and H. Cells with <50 per class are marked 'descriptive'. A logistic slope test of judge correctness vs words: judge_cheap_disg correct at threshold ~ z(words) + label.\\n  SYSTEM LEVEL: per Logic-LM system, the true error rate (labels) vs each metric's flagged rate and mean score. With 3 systems this is descriptive only; no τ test.\\n  COVERAGE: per metric, the fraction of items scored without failure, by track and system; the UNPARSEABLE count per system; the label-class distribution per system (CORRECT/ERROR/UNCERTAIN/UNPARSEABLE) and the vocab_borderline count.\\n  COST: per metric, the mean $ per item (from costs.jsonl) and seconds per item (wall time per item for LLM calls; the GPU NLI batch time amortised per item; z3 CPU seconds).\\n\\nSTEP 10: OUTPUTS\\n  results/method_out.json: follow the aii-json exp_gen_sol_out schema (validate it; generate the mini/preview variants). Each example carries the per-item fields {item_id, track, system, label, auto_class, repair_ops, census_class, strata, parse_ok, pilot_joint_conflict, pilot_story_unsat, pilot_arity_incons, pilot_dangling, pilot_undeclared, pilot_rerun_jacc, rt_verbalisation, rt_nli_min, rt_nli_fwd, rt_nli_bwd, rt_nli_contra, rt_embed_cos, rt_reformalise_eq, judge_cheap_orig, judge_cheap_disg, judge_cheap_type, judge_cheap2_orig, judge_cheap2_disg, judge_strong_orig, judge_strong_disg, best_baseline_oof, fold, cost_usd, seconds, coverage_status}. A top-level 'analysis' block holds every table above, plus match rates, the labeller regression diff, disguise audit stats, the prereg sha1 and total spend.\\n  Also results/rewrite_scores.jsonl, results/tables/*.csv, results/summary.md (a plain-language table of AUROC [CI] per baseline, the contamination verdict, the best-combo AUROC and the bar statement: 'candidates must beat judge_cheap_disg AUROC=X and best_baseline_oof AUROC=Y').\\n  Write README.md and .aii/manifest.yaml (keep: results/, data/; delete (redownloadable): the HF model cache hf_cache/ with source 'huggingface-cli download MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-large-mnli-fever-anli-ling-wanli sentence-transformers/all-mpnet-base-v2 cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-large'; .venv/ regenerable via 'uv sync'). Set HF_HOME=./hf_cache so the cache stays inside the workspace.\",\n  \"fallback_plan\": \"F1, Logic-LM download fails: try git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM, then the GitHub contents API. If all fail, track L cannot be built. Then run everything on track H only, mark 'track L unavailable' at the top of summary.md, and still emit screen_items.json with track H, so the label vector stays joinable.\\nF2, low match rate: if fewer than 60 track-L CORRECT+ERROR items remain after labelling (likely if Logic-LM's coarse predicates push many items into UNCERTAIN), do NOT add other sources (strategy rule). Instead (i) still score every UNCERTAIN item, so dataset E's adjudicated labels can re-admit them in iteration 2; (ii) report analysis (c) (ERROR∪UNCERTAIN vs CORRECT) prominently, labelled pessimistic; (iii) make track H the powered track for this artifact and say so.\\nF3, OpenRouter model unavailable or price changed: use the pre-listed fallbacks (primary: gemini-2.5-flash-lite → gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001 → qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct; secondary: gpt-4.1-nano → gpt-5-nano with reasoning minimal → mistral-small with a score output; strong: gpt-5 low → gemini-2.5-pro → gemini-3-pro-preview). Record the swap in prereg.json BEFORE running.\\nF4, budget pressure: the priority order when a cap binds is (1) primary cheap judge orig+disg on track L, (2) the same on track H, (3) the strong judge on the subset (shrink it before dropping it), (4) round-trip on track L, (5) the secondary cheap judge, (6) rewrites (score them with the primary judge only), (7) the gold-recall probe. Stop at $9.5 total whatever remains, and list the skipped components.\\nF5, the JSON-score format fails more than 5% for the primary judge after retries: switch to the logprob YES/NO format with the same model if it supports logprobs. Otherwise parse a bare integer 0-100 from the text. Failures stay flagged and counted.\\nF6, GPU unavailable or OOM: run NLI on CPU (≈2k pairs × 2 directions with DeBERTa-large at ~0.3 s/pair ≈ 20 min with 7 vCPUs), or drop to MoritzLaurer/DeBERTa-v3-base-mnli-fever-anli and log it.\\nF7, the disguise assertions fail on more than 5% of items: fall back to formula-only disguise for predicate names and a text disguise restricted to tokens whose lemma matches a predicate piece. Report the reduced leak coverage. Never silently judge a mis-disguised item: failed items are flagged.\\nF8, the pilot zip cannot be read or holds no clear definitions: use the definitions in Step 8 as written in the artifact direction, and say so in pilot_mapping.md.\\nF9, z3 timeouts on the joint-load or labelling checks: 5 s per check → UNKNOWN, reported as a coverage failure. Never merge UNKNOWN with UNSAT or EQUIV.\\nF10, running short of time (6 h): drop the rewrite scoring for round-trip (keep the judges), drop the alt NLI checkpoint, and cut the bootstrap to 1,000 resamples. The primary track-L table, contamination and best-combination outputs must be produced first.\",\n  \"testing_plan\": \"T1, parser/labeller (≤15 min): unit tests for the fixed precedence (⊕ inside →, right-assoc →, quantifier-binding convention). Rerun the census on the 99 track-H pairs: expect ≈21 VOCAB/GRAN and the 3 ⊕/→ bracketing COMPOUNDs to change; any other diff must be explained in logs. equivalent_modulo_vocab(x, x) == EQUIV on 50 random gold formulas, and a renamed copy → VOCAB.\\nT2, Logic-LM parsing: print 5 parsed programs per system. Assert that each has a conclusion line and that the 'FOL ::: NL' split succeeds on ≥95% of lines. Report the per-system parse rate and the conclusion match rate BEFORE any scoring. Hand-check 10 matched pairs in the log (text equal, reference correct).\\nT3, screen integrity: item_ids are unique; the label counts per track/system are printed; label_vector.sha1 is written; rerunning screen.py gives an identical sha1 (determinism).\\nT4, rewrites: 100% of the kept rewrites are z3-equivalent to their base; the per-family applicability counts are logged.\\nT5, disguise: the assertions (i)-(v) pass on all items; 30 audit items are printed; the leak rate in the formula is 0.\\nT6, judge mini-run (20 items, orig+disg, both cheap judges): the JSON/logprob parse succeeds on ≥19/20; the measured $/call is extrapolated to the full sweep and checked against the caps; sanity: on 10 gold-identical CORRECT items vs 10 obvious perturbations (a negated consequent), the mean score gap should exceed 0.3. The strong-judge dry run: 10 calls, measured cost and validity.\\nT7, round-trip sanity: NLI(text, text) entailment > 0.9 on 20 items; verbalising and re-formalising a gold formula returns EQUIV/VOCAB on ≥50% of 20 items (if lower, log it: that is a baseline weakness, not a bug, unless parse failures dominate).\\nT8, analysis sanity: shuffled labels give AUROC ≈0.5 (±0.05) for every metric; the AUROC of a perfect oracle (the label itself) = 1.0; the bootstrap CIs contain the point estimate; the GroupKFold folds never split a sentence.\\nT9, staged scaling (aii-long-running-tasks): mini (20) → 100 → full for every LLM sweep. Check the elapsed time and spend at each stage against the 6 h and $9.5 envelopes before growing.\\nT10, final checks: the method_out.json schema validates with aii-json; total spend in costs.jsonl ≤ $9.5 and it matches budget_state.json; every item in screen_items.json has a row in method_out.json (coverage_status set, none missing).\"\n}\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

Reference-free factuality and faithfulness metric papers (TRUE, SummaC, FRANK, and the autoformalization-alignment line FormalAlign and 2604.19459) always report: (1) an LLM-as-judge with a fixed rubric, G-Eval style, often at two model sizes; (2) an NLI-entailment metric (SummaC/TRUE use a DeBERTa/T5 NLI model on source vs output); (3) an embedding or n-gram similarity (BERTScore/cosine), for NL->FOL applied after back-translation; (4) the trivial 'compiles/parses' baseline; (5) in autoformalization, back-translation or round-trip consistency and sampling self-consistency. The one a reviewer names first if missing is the LLM judge. The fair-tuning norm: a fixed prompt, temperature 0, an output format that yields a continuous score (logprob of the verdict token, or a 0-100 score, NOT only a binary verdict, so AUROC is not tie-crippled), and prompt choice on a dev slice that is disjoint from the evaluation items. The strongest version of the baseline also includes a frontier-model judge, because a cheap-judge-only bar is a straw man.\\n\\nDATA. FOLIO and MALLS are the standard NL->FOL gold sets. As shipped they are ~39%/36% wrong (2606.02837), so the field now uses corrected releases (DSAVlab-UNIUD) or double-annotated agreement subsets (yfxiao/folio-refined). FOLIO is public and heavily memorised, which is why disguised or perturbed items are the standard contamination control (entity or predicate renaming, as in 2604.19459-style manipulation tests). Logic-LM's released outputs are the standard, reproducible 'real system outputs' for FOLIO, but they come from deprecated models (text-davinci-003) and FOLIO v1 text. ProverQA is synthetic and has no long conditioned sentences (iter-3 probe).\\n\\nCONTROLS. Same items, same labels and same item_id for every metric (paired design). Judge prompt, temperature and model are fixed before labels are seen. Disguise must be consistent (same lemma → same nonce in text and formula) and must preserve arity and argument order, otherwise the disguised judge is scored on a different task. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: a disguise-induced accuracy drop is read as contamination when it is really lost lexical semantics (disguise makes the task harder). The field's answer is a control condition in which memorisation is impossible, hence the difference-in-differences design below. The second: self-preference or family overlap between judge and generator (GPT judge on GPT outputs), or between judge and label adjudicator.\\n\\nHOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Item-level AUROC with ~100 positives and ~100 negatives has an SE of ≈0.03. A paired ΔAUROC of about 0.05 is detectable at ~300/300. Reviewers distrust per-stratum AUROCs with fewer than ~50 items per class. Point estimates come with 95% bootstrap CIs (≥1,000, usually 2,000 resamples) or DeLong CIs, clustered when items share a source sentence. Precision is always reported with its prevalence. With only 3 generator systems, system-level Kendall τ has no power (n=3) and is descriptive only.\\n\\nMEASURES AND REPORTING. ROC AUC (threshold-free, primary) and AUPRC with its prevalence baseline. At a pre-registered threshold: balanced accuracy, precision, recall and false-alarm rate. For binary or quantized metrics: tie rate and tie-aware pairwise accuracy (Ties Matter). Coverage, with metric failures scored as 'flagged' and counted. Cost in $ and seconds per item. In fol.py set PREC = {'iff':1,'xor':3,'imp':2,'or':3,'and':4}, so ⊕ binds like ∨, tighter than →.\\n  Unit tests: parse('A(x) ⊕ B(x) → C(x)') == ('imp',('xor',A,B),C); parse('A → B → C') is right-assoc; parse('∀x (P(x)) ∧ ∀y (Q(y))') gives two quantified conjuncts.\\n  labeller.py:\\n    def equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand_str, ref_str, ms=5000) -> dict:\\n      try parse both, else return {cls:'UNPARSEABLE'}\\n      r = equivalent(c, ref); if r is True: return {cls:'EQUIV'}; if r is None: remember timeout\\n      ao, pmap, cmap = align(c, ref); if equivalent(ao, ref) is True: return {cls:'VOCAB', pmap, cmap, borderline: any(token_jaccard(p,q) < 0.5 for renames)}\\n      gb = gran_bridge(ao, ref); if gb and equivalent(*gb) is True: return {cls:'GRAN', borderline: True}\\n      ops, dt = search(gb[0] if gb else ao, gb[1] if gb else ref, budget_s=25)\\n      if ops: return {cls:'ERROR', ops, depth:len(ops)}\\n      return {cls:'TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN'} if every equivalence call timed out else {cls:'COMPOUND'}\\n  Regression check: rerun on the 99 track-H non-equivalent pairs and diff against iter-3 repair_census.rows.json. Log every changed row, expecting the ⊕/→ bracketing COMPOUNDs to change. Save results/labeller_regression.json.\\n  Run all labelling in a ProcessPoolExecutor (spawn, 6 workers, per-item wall timeout of 60 s → TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN).\\n\\nSTEP 2: SHARED SCREEN (src/screen.py), exactly as the strategy defines it\\n  norm(s) = lowercase, unify quotes/dashes, strip all punctuation, collapse whitespace.\\n  download the 3 Logic-LM files from raw.githubusercontent.com/teacherpeterpan/Logic-LLM/main/outputs/logic_programs/FOLIO_dev_{sys}.json (fallback: git clone --depth 1, or the GitHub contents API). Save them under data/logiclm/.\\n  parse_program(raw_logic_programs[0]): split on the 'Predicates:', 'Premises:' and 'Conclusion:' headers. Each line splits at ' ::: ' into (fol, nl). Keep the predicate glosses in the record, NOT shown to judges. A malformed program → every sentence of that story gets candidate_fol=None → UNPARSEABLE.\\n  curated conclusions: rows whose id starts with 'concl_' → {nl, ref=FOL_sentence, orig=FOL_sentence_old, amb=ambiguity}.\\n  TRACK L conclusions: for each (sys, story, conclusion line) whose norm(nl) equals the norm of a curated conclusion → reference = corrected FOL_sentence.\\n  TRACK L premises: for each Logic-LM premise, find a curated story premise with an equal norm(nl). Split the curated story NL paragraph into sentences with a regex splitter; align sentence i to FOL_sentence_old line i ONLY when the counts match (log the stories where they do not). Also look up the folio-refined 'nl premises'/'fol premises' line with the same norm(nl). Accept it as a trusted reference only if equivalent_modulo_vocab(orig_line, refined_line) ∈ {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}; reference = the original line.\\n  MATCH-RATE rule: report n_distinct_conclusions_matched, n_premises_matched and the rates. Include premise items only as the strategy specifies: ALWAYS include matched agreed premises as track-L items with ref_type='agreed_premise' (the strategy's track-L definition includes them). If fewer than 150 distinct conclusions matched, ALSO add the matched stories' agreed premises (the same set, so the rule is automatically satisfied), and log the case. Use no other source.\\n  label track L: eq = equivalent_modulo_vocab(cand, ref) → label = CORRECT if eq.cls in {EQUIV, VOCAB, GRAN}; ERROR if eq.cls=='ERROR'; UNCERTAIN if eq.cls in {COMPOUND, TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN}; UNPARSEABLE if eq.cls=='UNPARSEABLE'. Store auto_class, repair_ops and vocab_borderline.\\n  TRACK H: the 202 curated conclusions + 100 MALLS-test rows; candidate = FOL_sentence_old, reference = corrected, system='human_original', and the sub-source kept.\\n    If equivalent(orig, ref) → CORRECT. Otherwise run eq as above. If amb is True and non-equivalent modulo vocab → READING_CHOICE. Otherwise map as in track L (VOCAB/GRAN → CORRECT_VOCAB, a sub-flag of CORRECT that is also reported separately). Orig unparseable → UNPARSEABLE.\\n    The census class for per-type sensitivity is eq.ops (single-op/two-op) or COMPOUND.\\n  item_id = sha1(system + '|' + norm(text) + '|' + candidate_fol_raw.strip())[:16]\\n  strata, computed from the TEXT and the REFERENCE formula (candidate-independent): words = len(text.split()) → bin '<12' | '12-19' | '>=20'; n_quant = count of ∀/∃ in the reference; depth = AST depth of the reference; n_cond = number of conjuncts in the antecedents of → and in the restrictors of ∀ in the reference; exception = regex r'\\\\b(unless|except|excluding|other than|apart from|but not|without)\\\\b' on the text, storing the marker.\\n  story context (for the pilot metrics): the same system's premises for the story (track L); the curated FOL_sentence_old lines for the story (FOLIO track H); none for MALLS.\\n  write data/screen_items.json, sorted by item_id: {item_id, track, system, story_id, role, text, candidate_fol, reference_fol, ref_type, label, auto_class, repair_ops, vocab_borderline, ambiguity, census_class, strata, story_premises_fol}.\\n  Also write data/label_vector.sha1 = sha1 of the sorted 'item_id:label' lines, so iteration 2 can check byte-identity with experiments A/B/C.\\n\\nSTEP 3: INVARIANCE SET (src/rewrites.py)\\n  base = the first 150 track-L CORRECT items by item_id (if fewer than 150 exist, use all and top up with track-H CORRECT items by item_id, logged).\\n  families, seed 0:\\n    RENAME: each predicate and constant → a WordNet synonym of its head word (else the name + 'Thing'), same arity. Verify with equivalence under the inverse map.\\n    REORDER: shuffle the conjuncts/disjuncts of every ∧/∨ node.\\n    DEMORGAN: rewrite one ¬(A∧B) ↔ ¬A∨¬B, or introduce ¬¬ on an ∧ node.\\n    PRENEX: pull quantifiers out where the variables do not clash.\\n    CONTRAPOSITIVE: A→B becomes ¬B→¬A.\\n  Keep a rewrite only if it is z3-equivalent to its base (RENAME: modulo the known map) and its string differs. Record per-family applicability counts. Rewrites are scored by every baseline, including the disguised judge; they need no labels.\\n  write data/invariance_items.json {rw_id, base_item_id, family, text, candidate_fol}.\\n\\nSTEP 4: DISGUISE (src/disguise.py)\\n  PROTECTED = logic, quantifier and function words: all, every, each, some, any, no, none, not, never, if, then, only, unless, except, either, or, and, nor, both, neither, is, are, be, a, an, the, who, which, that, there, exists, also, may, can, must, does, do, of, in, on, at, to, for, with, by, from, as, than, more, most, least, many, few, one, two, three, their, they, it, its, he, she, his, her, someone, something, everyone, everything, people, person, thing (the last four are kept: sortal nouns).\\n  content lemmas: spaCy en_core_web_sm POS in {NOUN, PROPN, VERB, ADJ, ADV} and not in PROTECTED, plus the camelCase/underscore pieces of every predicate and constant name in the candidate (lowercase, lemmatised with spaCy's lemmatizer).\\n  nonce(lemma, seed_text) = a pronounceable CV(C)CV(C)CV string from a fixed syllable inventory (consonants 'bdfgklmnprstvz', vowels 'aeiou'), indexed by int(sha1(norm(seed_text)+'|'+lemma),16). Reject and re-hash if the string is an English word (nltk words corpus) or collides with another lemma in the item.\\n    seed_text = norm(text), so the same sentence gets the same mapping in all 3 Logic-LM systems' items and in its rewrites.\\n  text: replace each content token by its nonce, carrying the inflection over by simple suffix rules (-s, -es, -ed, -ing, -er, -est, -ly). Capitalise it if the token was capitalised (proper nouns).\\n  formula: predicate name → CamelCase concatenation of the mapped pieces, with protected pieces kept as-is (e.g. PerformOftenIn → BlikorTadumIn). Constant → lowercase concatenation. Arity, argument order, variables and connectives are untouched. Operate on the AST, re-serialise with a printer that round-trips through fol.parse.\\n  ASSERTIONS, run on ALL items; the 30 sha1-first items are also printed to results/disguise_audit.txt:\\n    (i) the disguised formula parses, and applying the inverse name map gives an AST identical to the original's;\\n    (ii) the multiset of arities is unchanged;\\n    (iii) same lemma → same nonce in text and formula; distinct lemmas → distinct nonces;\\n    (iv) residual leak rate = the fraction of original content lemmas (length ≥4) still present in the disguised text or formula, which must be 0 for the formula; the text rate is reported;\\n    (v) every PROTECTED word count in the text is unchanged.\\n  A failing item keeps judge_*_disg = metric failure (flagged) and is counted.\\n  write data/disguise_map.json.\\n\\nSTEP 5: PRE-REGISTRATION (prereg.json, written and sha1-hashed BEFORE any score is joined to a track-L label)\\n  contents: model ids and prices; prompt texts and their sha1; temperature 0; max_tokens; the strong-judge subsample item_ids; the score orientation of every metric (higher = more likely unfaithful); the operating thresholds; the baseline combination feature sets; the bootstrap settings (2,000 resamples, cluster = norm(text), seed 0); the tie-margin and stratum-testability rule (≥50 per class, otherwise 'descriptive only'); the vocab_borderline sensitivity analysis.\\n  Operating thresholds, fixed on TRACK H CORRECT items only (never track L):\\n    judges: flag if P(faithful) < 0.5;\\n    continuous non-LLM or round-trip metrics: flag if the score is worse than the 10th percentile of their values on track-H CORRECT items (a 10% FA target);\\n    binary metrics: their natural value.\\n\\nSTEP 6: JUDGES (src/judges.py)\\n  PRIMARY CHEAP JUDGE (the pre-registered bar 'judge_cheap'): google/gemini-2.5-flash-lite (fallbacks in order: google/gemini-2.0-flash-lite-001, qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-instruct-2507, verified with aii-openrouter-llms). A non-OpenAI family avoids self-preference on GPT-generated track-L candidates.\\n  SECONDARY CHEAP JUDGE: openai/gpt-4.1-nano with logprobs=True, top_logprobs=5, max_tokens=1. It uses a minimal variant of the same rubric ending 'Answer with one word: YES if faithful, NO otherwise.' P(faithful) = exp(lp_YES)/(exp(lp_YES)+exp(lp_NO)), summed over the case/space variants of YES/NO. If logprobs are absent, fall back to the verdict (0/1) and log it.\\n  RUBRIC (system prompt, fixed): 'You check whether a first-order logic formula faithfully expresses the meaning of an English sentence. Variant B appends the census definitions: NEG negation wrong/missing; REV implication direction reversed; QUANT wrong quantifier; RESTR restrictor misplaced (e.g. ∀x(A∧B) where ∀x(A→B) is meant, or ∃x(A→B)); CONN wrong connective (∧/∨/⊕/→/↔); MOVE content attached to the wrong part; DROP a condition or content missing; ADD content not in the sentence; SWAP arguments in the wrong order; BIND variable wrongly bound or free; SCOPE quantifier scope wrong; UNGLUE independent claims wrongly merged or split; COMPOUND several of these; NONE.\\n  User message: 'Sentence: {text}\\\\nFormula: {fol}\\\\nReturn JSON: {\\\"faithful_prob\\\": <0-100>, \\\"verdict\\\": \\\"FAITHFUL\\\"|\\\"UNFAITHFUL\\\", \\\"error_type\\\": <one code from the list, NONE if faithful>, \\\"reason\\\": \\\"<=25 words\\\"}'. Primary judge: response_format json_object where supported, temperature 0, max_tokens 150.\\n  PROMPT DEV (the fair-tuning step): run variants A and B of the primary judge on a 60-item dev slice = the 60 sha1-first MALLS track-H items (CORRECT + ERROR). Each item is run orig AND disg (600 calls).\\n    If the dry-run $/call × 600 > $5.0: first lower the reasoning effort ('minimal') or the thinking budget; then shrink to 150 L + 70 H. Always keep the orig/disg pairing.\\n  GOLD-RECALL PROBE (contamination, direct): the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this sentence into first-order logic in FOLIO notation: {text}' for every track-H FOLIO conclusion and MALLS item (302 calls, ≈$0.03). Measure: the rate of z3-equivalence to the ORIGINAL public gold vs to the corrected gold, and the exact predicate-name-set Jaccard with the original gold vs with the Logic-LM gpt-4 output for the same sentence. Name overlap far above the Logic-LM baseline indicates memorised gold.\\n  per-item fields: judge_cheap_orig, judge_cheap_disg (P(faithful) in [0,1]), judge_cheap_type, judge_cheap_type_disg, judge_cheap2_orig/disg, judge_strong_orig/disg, judge_*_fail flags, cost_usd, seconds.\\n\\nSTEP 7: ROUND-TRIP (src/roundtrip.py)\\n  verbalise: the primary cheap model gets 'Translate this first-order logic formula into one plain English sentence. CIs: cluster bootstrap over norm(text), 2,000 resamples, seed 0, percentile 95%.\\n  SECONDARY: (a) track H (dev slice excluded), CORRECT vs ERROR with READING_CHOICE and UNCERTAIN excluded; (b) track L with vocab_borderline moved to UNCERTAIN; (c) track L ERROR ∪ UNCERTAIN vs CORRECT (a pessimistic label view); (d) the strong-judge subset, where every metric is recomputed on those same items so the comparisons are paired.\\n  PAIRED DELTAS: ΔAUROC for judge_cheap_disg vs every other metric, and judge_strong_orig vs judge_cheap_orig/disg on the subset (cluster bootstrap on the same resamples).
